title:PvE Podcast 177 - The Best Trace Rifles
description:Monument of Triumph new and reprised Trace Rifles, based on trait perk discussions between @CourtProjects and @ImpetusAlways in the 4 August 2026 @PodvsEnemies podcast (https://open.spotify.com/episode/4AemBHDclMhu14RT88A1rG). Destiny Data Compendium, Aegis's Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​ and other resources, available at https://www.destiny2.science/, or through https://www.youtube.com/@TheAegisRelic. The effect of weapon perks on stats can be found at https://destiny.report or https://d2foundry.gg 

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Rimestealer, Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Crystalline Corpsebloom, Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Stasis synergy, in the first trait perk column, Rimestealer generates ×2 stacks of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy. There's also perks to help delay the need to reload - Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a large Stasis crystal or Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. These crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Killing Tally, Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column to delay the need to reload, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow or, if you're using a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Detonator Beam)

Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column to delay the need to reload, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow or, if you're using a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Rimestealer, Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Crystalline Corpsebloom, Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Stasis synergy, in the first trait perk column, Rimestealer generates ×2 stacks of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy. There's also perks to help delay the need to reload - Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a large Stasis crystal or Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. These crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 3
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Detonator Beam)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column to delay the need to reload, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow or, if you're using a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Rimestealer, Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Crystalline Corpsebloom, Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Stasis synergy, in the first trait perk column, Rimestealer generates ×2 stacks of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy. There's also perks to help delay the need to reload - Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a large Stasis crystal or Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. These crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller

// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Killing Tally, Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column to delay the need to reload, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow or, if you're using a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Rimestealer, Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Crystalline Corpsebloom, Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Stasis synergy, in the first trait perk column, Rimestealer generates ×2 stacks of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy. There's also perks to help delay the need to reload - Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a large Stasis crystal or Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. These crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Killing Tally, Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column to delay the need to reload, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow or, if you're using a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Rimestealer, Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Crystalline Corpsebloom, Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Stasis synergy, in the first trait perk column, Rimestealer generates ×2 stacks of Stasis Frost Armor on shattering a Stasis crystal or killing a frozen enemy. There's also perks to help delay the need to reload - Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column, Crystalline Corpsebloom procs on a kill to provide a 4s buff - kills with the buff active generate a Stasis seeker which travels 3m before spawning a large Stasis crystal or Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill. These crystals are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller

// Action Item - PvE Minor god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Demolitionist, Rewind Rounds), (Killing Tally, Elemental Honing)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Action Item is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's available from Iron Banner if you fancy a bit of PvP. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty much perfect, so you can look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range with the barrel perk. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column to delay the need to reload, Demolitionist generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use, or Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots and delaying the need to reload. In the second column for damage, Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow or, if you're using a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Aegis ranks the Nail, Meet Hammer origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it grants ×1.08 damage against shields, and an additional 8% Heavy Ammo progress when initially breaking the shield. There also the D-Tier Omolon Fluid Dynamics which boosts Stability by up to +20 and Reload Speed by +30 when the battery's full, decreasing linearly until the mag's at 50%. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Retraced Path - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Shoot to Loot), (Incandescent)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame Solar-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Solar builds. Retraced Path is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Dare of Eternity loot pool, if you're looking for one, and is shapable if you unlock the pattern. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For endgame use with a bit of Solar synergy, Shoot to Loot lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves, while Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+45 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments and weapon traits like Shoot to Loot. All of the scorch stacks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Aegis ranks the Hot Swap origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - on a weapon kill you gain ×2 stacks up to ×6 max, you then use the stacks when readying or stowing for +100 Handling with a ×0.65 ready/stow animation scalar. If you're interested, the name is a double pun: signifying both a "Trace Rifle" and Bungie "retracing its steps" back Halo: Reach, modelling the weapon on the Covenant Focus Rifle for the Destiny 2 30th Anniversary Pack. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame Solar-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Acasia's Dejection - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Burning Ambition), (Incandescent)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame Solar-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Solar builds. Acasia's Dejection is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Root of Nightmares loot pool, if you're looking for one, and is shapable if you unlock the pattern. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Solar damage verb synergy, you'll need a Solar subclass with a scorch ability to make the most of Burning Ambition in the first trait perk column to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets. If you can't apply scorch first, it'll take ~52 hits within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks, with +20 added on each additional hit afterwards to build towards an ignition. In the second column, Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+45 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments. All the scorch stacks of both perks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - each additional Root of Nightmares weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting Handling and Reload Speed, and applying damage boosts of ×1.033 for one or ×1.1 for two, against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers. If you're interested, Acasia was a Psion whose partner was taken to be tortured as a sacrifice to Nezarec, Final God of Pain. She joined Nezarec's Acolytes in the desperate hope of freeing her lover, making "Dejection" reflect her profound grief and disillusionment. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame Solar-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

The scorch stacks of are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Alternatively in the first column, to delay the need to reload, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 5.5s up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. In the second column for damage, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 11.5s - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration - although this is bugged, so while it's active it interrupts natural recovery and prevents regenerating the final 20-25% of your health bar. Alternatively Frenzy, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s, until you're out of combat again, or Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works well on overflowed mags or with perks that delay the need to reload. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - each additional Root of Nightmares weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting Handling and Reload Speed, and applying damage boosts of ×1.033 for one or ×1.1 for two, against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers. If you're interested, Acasia was a Psion whose partner was taken to be tortured as a sacrifice to Nezarec, Final God of Pain. She joined Nezarec's Acolytes in the desperate hope of freeing her lover, making "Dejection" reflect her profound grief and disillusionment. // Acasia's Dejection (Adept) - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Burning Ambition), (Incandescent)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame Solar-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Solar builds. Acasia's Dejection is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Root of Nightmares loot pool, if you're looking for one, and is shapable if you unlock the pattern. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Solar damage verb synergy, you'll need a Solar subclass with a scorch ability to make the most of Burning Ambition in the first trait perk column to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets. If you can't apply scorch first, it'll take ~52 hits within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks, with +20 added on each additional hit afterwards to build towards an ignition. In the second column, Incandescent spreads +30 Solar Scorch stacks within 4m on a red bar Minor add kill (+45 stacks within 8m for orange and yellow bars), with scorch damage counting as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments. All the scorch stacks of both perks are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - each additional Root of Nightmares weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting Handling and Reload Speed, and applying damage boosts of ×1.033 for one or ×1.1 for two, against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers. If you're interested, Acasia was a Psion whose partner was taken to be tortured as a sacrifice to Nezarec, Final God of Pain. She joined Nezarec's Acolytes in the desperate hope of freeing her lover, making "Dejection" reflect her profound grief and disillusionment. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame Solar-Build PvE-God M+KB Controller

For double damage trait perks, you'll need a Solar subclass with a scorch ability to make the most of Burning Ambition in the first column to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets. If you can't apply scorch first, it'll take ~52 hits within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks, with +20 added on each additional hit afterwards to build towards an ignition. The scorch stacks of are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Alternatively in the first column, to delay the need to reload, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 5.5s up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. In the second column, Detonator Beam procs an 8m AoE Solar explosion after 20 hits - this counts as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts, and also proc subclass fragments. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - each additional Root of Nightmares weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting Handling and Reload Speed, and applying damage boosts of ×1.033 for one or ×1.1 for two, against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers. If you're interested, Acasia was a Psion whose partner was taken to be tortured as a sacrifice to Nezarec, Final God of Pain. She joined Nezarec's Acolytes in the desperate hope of freeing her lover, making "Dejection" reflect her profound grief and disillusionment. // Acasia's Dejection (Adept) - PvE Minor god 3
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Burning Ambition, Rewind Rounds, Reconstruction), (Chaos Reshaped, Frenzy, Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Acasia's Dejection is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Root of Nightmares loot pool, if you're looking for one, and is shapable if you unlock the pattern. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For double damage trait perks, you'll need a Solar subclass with a scorch ability to make the most of Burning Ambition in the first column to build Solar ignitions on tougher targets. If you can't apply scorch first, it'll take ~52 hits within 2s of each other to apply +20 scorch stacks, with +20 added on each additional hit afterwards to build towards an ignition. The scorch stacks of are increased by ×1.5 with Ember of Ashes. Alternatively in the first column, to delay the need to reload, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots, or Reconstruction refills 25% of the battery every 5.5s up to ×2 capacity when stowed or not firing. In the second column for damage, Chaos Reshaped stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 11.5s - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration - although this is bugged, so while it's active it interrupts natural recovery and prevents regenerating the final 20-25% of your health bar. Alternatively Frenzy, after being in combat for 12s - that's dealing or taking damage - boosts Handling and Reload Speed by +100 and boosts damage by ×1.15 for a refreshable 7.2s, until you're out of combat again, or Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity - so works well on overflowed mags or with perks that delay the need to reload. Aegis ranks the Harmonic Resonance origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - each additional Root of Nightmares weapon equipped adds an additional stack, each boosting Handling and Reload Speed, and applying damage boosts of ×1.033 for one or ×1.1 for two, against Dread and Lucent Hive Lightbearers. If you're interested, Acasia was a Psion whose partner was taken to be tortured as a sacrifice to Nezarec, Final God of Pain. She joined Nezarec's Acolytes in the desperate hope of freeing her lover, making "Dejection" reflect her profound grief and disillusionment. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Chronophage - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Shoot to Loot), (Destabilizing Rounds)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd Void-Build PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly for Void builds. Chronophage is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. It's part of Xûr's weekly loot pool, if you're looking for one. The Recoil Direction is already an almost perfect 99, so use the barrel perk for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For endgame use, in the first trait perk column Shoot to Loot lets you pick up remote ammo bricks or orbs from safety by shooting them, which also reloads all your weapons from reserves. In the second column, for Void build synergy, Destabilizing Rounds spreads Void Volatility on a kill in a 6.5m radius and provides Volatile Rounds for 5.25s. Aegis ranks the Radiolaria Transposer origin trait as A-Tier for PvE - three Minors or one Major or Boss enemy kill will result in a 3m AoE explosion on death, leaving a damaging pool of radiolaria for a few seconds. The damage is treated as weapon damage so inherits damage boosts and also procs weapon effects like picking up ammo bricks and power orbs with Shoot to Loot. If you're interested, the name's derived from Greek words χρόνος - "khronos" (time) and φαγεῖν - "phagein" (to devour), literally meaning "time-eater", and is used colloquially in French to mean "time-consuming". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd Void-Build PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

The Recoil Direction is already a perfect 100, so use the barrel perk for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For endgame use, there's the handy trait perk combination of Shoot to Loot and perks that extend the pickup radius. First, there's Detonator Beam which procs an 8m AoE Arc explosion after 20 hits. There's also Butterfly which procs after 1.4s ADSing to generate a 4.5m Arc explosion on a kill. Both explosions count as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts and also proc subclass fragments, as well as weapon traits like Shoot to Loot to pick up ammo bricks and power orbs, and reload all your weapons. Aegis ranks the Classy Contender origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it provides 6% Class ability energy on kills, which is useful if not game-changing. If you're interested, the name is derived from the ancient Greek "κεραυνός" - a lightning bolt or thunderbolt, and is also a classical epithet for Zeus as the wielder of lightning, rooted in a verb meaning "to shatter". // Keraunios - PvE Minor god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Overflow, Rewind Rounds, Supercharged Magazine, Trickle Charge), (Jolting Feedback, Rolling Storm)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Keraunios is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Sundered Doctrine Dungeon loot pool, if you're looking for one. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. It's obtainable from the Tenet of Bravery Tower vendor via Guardian Games Engram purchases. The Recoil Direction is already a perfect 100, so use the barrel perk for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Arc build synergy, there are a host of first trait perk column options to help delay the need to reload. Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling mag size for better DPS and extending ammo reserves for better total damage if you can keep hitting crits. There's also Overflow which refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick and Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots. For Arc builds, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the battery every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff's active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Trickle Charge refills 10% of the battery on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target. For applying Arc damage verbs, Jolting Feedback applies Arc Jolt after 17 hits (13 when amplified) to tougher enemies who can survive the weapon damage, triggering chain lightning on further hits for both target and AoE damage, or Rolling Storm generates ×1 stack of Arc Bolt Charge (×2 when Amplified) on a kill, to trigger a lightning bolt at ×10 stacks, also for both target and AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Classy Contender origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it provides 6% Class ability energy on kills, which is useful if not game-changing. If you're interested, the name is derived from the ancient Greek "κεραυνός" - a lightning bolt or thunderbolt, and is also a classical epithet for Zeus as the wielder of lightning, rooted in a verb meaning "to shatter". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Keraunios - PvE Minor god 3
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Fourth Time's the Charm, Overflow, Rewind Rounds, Supercharged Magazine, Trickle Charge), (Detonator Beam, Butterfly, Chain Reaction)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Keraunios is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Sundered Doctrine Dungeon loot pool, if you're looking for one. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. It's obtainable from the Tenet of Bravery Tower vendor via Guardian Games Engram purchases. The Recoil Direction is already a perfect 100, so use the barrel perk for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. There are a host of first trait perk column options to help delay the need to reload. Fourth Time's the Charm generates 2 shots from thin air after 4 crits within 3s of each other, almost doubling mag size for better DPS and extending ammo reserves for better total damage if you can keep hitting crits. There's also Overflow which refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick and Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots. For Arc builds, Supercharged Magazine refills 5% of the battery every 1.5s while you're Amplified or, after you sprint when Amplified to gain Speed Booster this accelerates to 5% every 0.25s while the buff's active, overflowing the magazine to up to ×2 capacity, or Trickle Charge refills 10% of the battery on receiving a stack of Arc Bolt Charge, and 20% when you discharge with a lightning bolt on a target. For damage, there's Detonator Beam which procs an 8m AoE Arc explosion after 20 hits, or Butterfly which procs after 1.4s ADSing to generate a 4.5m Arc explosion on a kill. Both explosions count as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts and also proc subclass fragments. There's also Chain Reaction which creates an Arc 2.4m AoE explosion on kills to help clear add groups. Aegis ranks the Classy Contender origin trait as C-Tier for PvE - it provides 6% Class ability energy on kills, which is useful if not game-changing. If you're interested, the name is derived from the ancient Greek "κεραυνός" - a lightning bolt or thunderbolt, and is also a classical epithet for Zeus as the wielder of lightning, rooted in a verb meaning "to shatter". Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Unsworn - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Shoot to Loot), (Detonator Beam)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Unsworn is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Sundered Doctrine Dungeon loot pool, if you're looking for one. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For endgame use, there's the handy trait perk combination of Shoot to Loot and Detonator Beam. Detonator Beam procs an 8m AoE Strand explosion after 20 hits. This counts as weapon damage so can inherit damage boosts and also proc subclass fragments, as well as weapon traits like Shoot to Loot to pick up ammo bricks and power orbs, and reload all your weapons. Aegis ranks the Subjugation origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - against Minor and Major every fourth hit explodes for 3m AoE damage, similar to explosive payload and counts as weapon damage for damage buffs. Against Champions, Minibosses or Bosses it inflicts Exhaust, reducing their damage output to ×0.75. If you're interested, the name signifies a deliberate rejection of previous allegiances, covenants, or the "sworn" duties of the past. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Unsworn - PvE Minor god 3
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Rewind Rounds, Subsistence), (Chaos Reshaped, Killing Tally, Elemental Honing, Target Lock)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing individual Minor adds or tougher enemies in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Unsworn is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Sundered Doctrine Dungeon loot pool, if you're looking for one. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk column, to delay the need to reload, Rewind Rounds refills 70% of hits landed from reserves when the battery empties, effectively increasing battery size by around ×3.0-3.3 if you hit all your shots, or Subsistence reloads 20% of the battery on kills. In the second column for damage, you can just about take your pick - if you're on a Prismatic build, Elemental Honing grants an escalating stack of damage up to max ×1.35 for 23s for each elemental damage type inflicted, which can be refreshed with a new damage type until you've run through all 5. Otherwise there's Chaos Reshaped which stacks up to ×2 on dealing or receiving damage within a 5s window for at least 11.5s - at ×1 it provides a ×1.2 damage boost for a refreshable 7s and then at ×2 provides ×1.35 damage for a refreshable 7s together with a healing burst of up to 35HP that also starts health regeneration - although this is bugged, so while it's active it interrupts natural recovery and prevents regenerating the final 20-25% of your health bar. Finally, there's Killing Tally which gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow, or Target Lock stacks up to 5 times on repeated hits within 0.2s of each other, for up to ×1.4 damage at 110.33% of base magazine capacity, both of which synergise well with the first column reloading perks. Aegis ranks the Subjugation origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - against Minor and Major every fourth hit explodes for 3m AoE damage, similar to explosive payload and counts as weapon damage for damage buffs. Against Champions, Minibosses or Bosses it inflicts Exhaust, reducing their damage output to ×0.75. If you're interested, the name signifies a deliberate rejection of previous allegiances, covenants, or the "sworn" duties of the past. // Unsworn - PvE Minor god 4
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Tear), (Hatchling)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-God Strand-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal difficulty, particularly with Strand builds. Unsworn is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's part of the Sundered Doctrine Dungeon loot pool, if you're looking for one. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. For Strand synergy, there's the combination of Tear and Hatchling, which both proc on a crit kill. Tear spreads ~4m AoE Strand Sever to reduce damage output to ×0.6 from enemies affected, while Hatchling spawns a Threadling on a crit kill or three crit hits within 3s of each other. Aegis ranks the Subjugation origin trait as S-Tier for PvE - against Minor and Major every fourth hit explodes for 3m AoE damage, similar to explosive payload and counts as weapon damage for damage buffs. Against Champions, Minibosses or Bosses it inflicts Exhaust, reducing their damage output to ×0.75. If you're interested, the name signifies a deliberate rejection of previous allegiances, covenants, or the "sworn" duties of the past. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Strand-Build M+KB Controller

// Appetence - PvE Minor god 1
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow, Demolitionist, Clown Cartridge), (Headstone)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty, particularly with Stasis builds. Appetence is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's accessible and shapable at the Mars Relic - even with the older perk pool it can still be useful. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk slot, to delay the need to reload, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick or Clown Cartridge overflows the battery by between ×1.13-1.5 randomly on a manual reload. There's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use. For Stasis synergy, in the second column Headstone generates a Stasis crystal on a crit kill - these are weapon-generated entities so can inherit damage buffs, and proc weapon traits and subclass fragments when shattered for AoE damage. Aegis ranks the Dragon's Vengeance origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it refills the battery from reserves, and grants +10 Range and +5 Handling with a ×0.975 Draw Time scalar for 11s after reaching critical health or on the death of an ally. If you're interested, the name's derived from the Latin "appetere" (to desire or reach for). "Appetence" means an intense natural desire, craving, or instinctive appetite, reflecting the Ahamkara's manipulation of desire during Season of the Wish. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-GroupAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God Stasis-Build M+KB Controller

// Appetence - PvE Minor god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Enhanced Battery, Tactical Battery, Light Battery), (Overflow, Demolitionist, Clown Cartridge), (Killing Tally, One For All)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for clearing groups of Minor adds in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Appetence is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's accessible and shapable at the Mars Relic - even with the older perk pool it can still be useful. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk slot, to delay the need to reload, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick or Clown Cartridge overflows the battery by between ×1.13-1.5 randomly on a manual reload. There's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use. For damage, in the second column Killing Tally gains a stack for each enemy killed up to ×3 to boost damage by up to ×1.3 until you reload or stow - so works best on overflowed mags or with perks that delay the need to reload, or One For All boosts damage by ×1.35 for a non-refreshable 11s after hitting three separate targets within 3s of each. Aegis ranks the Dragon's Vengeance origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it refills the battery from reserves, and grants +10 Range and +5 Handling with a ×0.975 Draw Time scalar for 11s after reaching critical health or on the death of an ally. If you're interested, the name's derived from the Latin "appetere" (to desire or reach for). "Appetence" means an intense natural desire, craving, or instinctive appetite, reflecting the Ahamkara's manipulation of desire during Season of the Wish. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Minor PvE-SingleAdd PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow, Demolitionist, Clown Cartridge), (Attrition Orbs)

Appetence is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's accessible and shapable at the Mars Relic - even with the older perk pool it can still be useful. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk slot, to delay the need to reload, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick or Clown Cartridge overflows the battery by between ×1.13-1.5 randomly on a manual reload. There's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use. For utility, in the second column Attrition Orbs spawns a power orb after ~54 hits within 5s of each other, to proc armour mods. Aegis ranks the Dragon's Vengeance origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it refills the battery from reserves, and grants +10 Range and +5 Handling with a ×0.975 Draw Time scalar for 11s after reaching critical health or on the death of an ally. If you're interested, the name's derived from the Latin "appetere" (to desire or reach for). "Appetence" means an intense natural desire, craving, or instinctive appetite, reflecting the Ahamkara's manipulation of desire during Season of the Wish. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller

// Appetence - PvE Utility god 2
// (Fluted Barrel, Arrowhead Brake, Corkscrew Rifling, Polygonal Rifling, Chambered Compensator, Extended Barrel, Smallbore, Hammer-Forged Rifling), (Tactical Battery, Light Battery, Enhanced Battery), (Overflow, Demolitionist, Clown Cartridge), (Deconstruct)
//notes:Constructed by @Adamsdown_Boy (PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller): First-choice PvE roll for tackling vehicles and constructs in activities rated Normal, Hard or Very Hard difficulty. Appetence is an adaptive trace rifle, which are rated A-Tier for endgame PvE by @TheAegisRelic Endgame Analysis Spreadsheet​, who says "high utility for shot count-sensitive use cases, Harsh Refraction makes damage profile competitive". It's accessible and shapable at the Mars Relic - even with the older perk pool it can still be useful. The frame intrinsic offers built in Anti-Barrier, but offers no bonuses beyond being "well-rounded, reliable and sturdy" as per the Destiny Data Compendium. The Recoil Direction is pretty good at base, so you can tidy that up to 100 with the barrel perk, or look for your preference of Handling, Stability or Range. For the battery perk, Tactical or Light Battery boost gunfeel stats or Enhanced Battery adds a few extra shots. In the first trait perk slot, to delay the need to reload, Overflow refills the battery and boosts its capacity to ×2.2 of base, rounded up, on picking up an ammo brick or Clown Cartridge overflows the battery by between ×1.13-1.5 randomly on a manual reload. There's also Demolitionist which generates chunk grenade ability energy on kills and an instant battery refill on grenade use. For encounters involving vehicles and constructs, Deconstruct grants ×1.4 damage against them and refills 10% of the battery from reserves after ~25 hits within 6s, or ~50 hits when the target isn't a vehicle or construct, delaying the need to reload. Aegis ranks the Dragon's Vengeance origin trait as D-Tier for PvE - it refills the battery from reserves, and grants +10 Range and +5 Handling with a ×0.975 Draw Time scalar for 11s after reaching critical health or on the death of an ally. If you're interested, the name's derived from the Latin "appetere" (to desire or reach for). "Appetence" means an intense natural desire, craving, or instinctive appetite, reflecting the Ahamkara's manipulation of desire during Season of the Wish. Recommended MW: Handling or Stability, with your preferred choice of mod to boost weapon stats, ammo generation, or elemental pickups for builds.|tags:PvE PvE-Utility PvE-EndGame PvE-God M+KB Controller
